# Meeting videos → Drive supporting documents

Built 2026-08-18 by walking the shared Drive folder [[Packets - Posted to website]](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1LXgVyI9nJCv4jDy73lAFT0XT47S5bdBK), including its `[Archived Packets]` subtree, and matching folder dates against the five videos in `Input/Videos/`. Dates come from the video filenames (M-D-YY, so `72126` = 7/21/26); for the three board meetings the match is corroborated by an explicitly dated Drive folder name. The shared [Meeting Minutes](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1482gj2MFrWIESHvadUpXEx5Tn_L3Vjdv) folder (year folders 2023–2027 plus `Unsealed Minutes`) is also scanned as of 2026-08-18, feeding the `Minutes:` lines below. The `Remote video:` lines link the same meetings' recordings in Claremont Community TV's Cablecast gallery [CLAREMONT SCHOOLS](https://reflect-claremont.cablecast.tv/internetchannel/gallery/6?site=1), matched 2026-08-19 and re-checked 2026-08-22. On 2026-08-23 the map was extended backwards to cover the 39 meetings of January 2025 – January 2026 that already had attributed dialogue in `Output/Dialogue/` but no local video: their Cablecast show IDs are the numeric prefixes of the transcript file names, each confirmed against the show's own metadata, and their packets were matched in the [2025 Meeting Documents](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1V6Dyb9Gr78OG7_myE1dmYza2Db6xTrc6) and [FY 2025-2026 Meeting Documents](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1-rBO5fyVoFsbGeoNZfevNcuaoAnxcFgk) archives. SAU 6 board meetings draw on a separate share, [Schoolboard Packets](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1NxqIs1xjT8073FMEW_qO5YWuLp7zwJN5), documented here for the first time. On 2026-08-26 the nine folders of that share that had never been opened — the five 2024 meetings, 8/21/25, 8/26/25, 4/9/26 and 5/28/26 — were inventoried and added as sections, which renumbered every later section; their Cablecast matches came from the site's own search API and the Meeting Minutes share was re-checked in full (2024–2027 year folders) and holds no SAU 6 file of any kind. On 2026-08-28 the map was extended backwards again, to the whole of February 2023 – December 2024 and the February – June 2026 gap: 74 meetings whose videos had arrived in `Input/Videos/` and whose dialogue CSVs had just been completed, matched against two archives opened here for the first time — [2023 Meeting Documents](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1stI0GTNhKp5ovBt9DZKvhaqHYjSWg_oL) (28 folders) and [2024 Meeting Documents](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1cLgG0fJDuHC8ft3h09j4aRWLbnCL37Fh) (21) — plus the `2023` and `2024 Meeting Packets` archives inside the SAU 6 share and the 2023, 2024 and 2026 Meeting Minutes year folders. That run also gave five already-mapped SAU 6 sections their local video files. The same day a Cablecast pass added `Remote video:` links to all 74, every one confirmed against the show's own `/CablecastAPI/v1/shows/<id>` metadata rather than its gallery title.

Last updated 2026-09-26 (full map update); 2026-08-28 (Drive and Cablecast); 2026-09-02: local video files recorded for the 39 January 2025 – January 2026 meetings downloaded on 2026-09-01/02 (19 of them renamed to match their transcripts; show 16872's file had carried Cablecast's wrong 9/26/25 eventDate), and dialogue CSVs recorded for the five mid-2026 meetings. 2026-09-24 (Drive): sections added for 1/3, 1/9, 1/11 and 1/17/23 and for 9/2 and 9/16/26 (every later section renumbered by four); 21 late documents added to the 8/19/26 packet table; unsealed 8/5/26 nonpublic minutes linked; finance-committee negatives for 6/19 and 8/12/26 re-confirmed. Same day (Cablecast): `Remote video:` lines added for those six sections, all confirmed by `eventDate` (shows 14822, 14835, 14833, 14847, 17575, 17574, 17594); the four SAU 6 meetings still without a recording (12/3/24, 8/21/25, 8/26/25, 5/28/26) were re-checked by scanning show ids around each date, with no match. 2026-09-26 (full map update, Drive and Cablecast): 180 sections added for the 2015 – 2022 meetings whose videos, transcripts and attributed dialogue were on hand (every later section renumbered by 180); the [2022 Meeting Documents](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1b1uvfbrRGH9_iyMTt9NTvKqabXNjyy9M) archive (10 folders) and the SAU 6 [2022 Meeting Packets](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1NnE26Z6CRFC1kvc6DGTGNiJCThXACf5O) archive (5 folders) were opened for the first time and supply packets for ten of them; the other 170 have no Drive material because neither share reaches back before July 2022. Every one of the 180 carries a `Remote video:` line whose show id is the file-name prefix, confirmed against `/CablecastAPI/v1/shows/<id>?site=1`; ten `eventDate` disagreements are recorded in the sections' date notes. Transcript notes were added to the four January 2023 sections.

Meetings are listed chronologically.

CSB 9.20.23`) carries only the 9.20 and 10.4 drafts, and a quoted-date search returns nothing in the district shares — verified 2026-08-28. The packet folder itself is unusually rich: it holds two sets of written questions submitted in advance by individual board members, with written answers from the Director of Special Education (B. Nester) and the Stevens High School principal (C. Pratt). **Correction, 2026-08-29:** an earlier version of this note called them "superintendent candidates". They were not — there was no search. Michael Tempesta was superintendent until January 2024 and gave this meeting's superintendent report by telephone. **Also: the recording is TRUNCATED.** The archived video runs 0:52:52 against a 6:30–8:30 p.m. agenda and stops during discussion item 2 of 7, so the board-governance discussion, two bargaining slates, the subcommittee round, the athletic-fields licence and a nonpublic session have no public record of any kind — and this meeting also has no minutes.

Video: `Input/Videos/15455 SAU6120723.mp4` (transcript also on hand: `Input/Transcripts/15455 SAU6120723.mp4.json`; dialogue: `Output/HTML/Dialogue/15455 SAU6120723.mp4.CSV`) Remote video: [SAU 6 Board Meeting 12/7/23](https://reflect-claremont.cablecast.tv/internetchannel/show/15455?site=1) Drive folder: [15. SAU6 12.7.23](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1bmx6IXVxm0DF0lXWWa9rF3Zlp2bDOPJp) — 3 documents *(in the SAU 6 Schoolboard Packets)*

**The minutes of this meeting EXIST, were approved, and are in no public share — verified exhaustively 2026-08-29.** The 12/14/23 draft minutes record the item "Approval of Minutes 12.7.23", the chair's floor correction ("page 2 under non-public, in the vote on the motion to seal minutes there was an abstention from Marjorie Erickson" — the abstention is audible at 0:30:26 of this recording), and "Rocco Ruggeri moved to approve the minutes as amended, Atonya Hart seconded; unanimously approved." So a document was written, corrected and adopted; it was simply never filed anywhere the public can reach.

The search behind this is now complete, not partial: **every one of the 19 SAU 6 folders dated after the meeting** was opened and enumerated — `16. SAU6 12.14.23` through `14. SAU6 5.28.26`, including two an earlier API-based inventory missed (`4. SAU Retreat 5.29.24`, and the 2023 archive's true count of 16 folders rather than 9) — plus the packets-share root and the Claremont Meeting Minutes share's 2023 and 2024 year folders (46 files, every one a CSB document). **December 7 is the single break in this board's filing chain**: 11/9 was filed in the 12/7 folder, 12/14 in `1. SAU 1.11.24`, 1/11/24 in `2. SAU 2.15.24`, 2/15/24 in `3. SAU 4.11.24`, and 4/11/24 and 5/29/24 in `1. SAU6 9.12.24`.

Folder `9. CSB 10.6.25` holds exactly one document, `CSB Agenda 10.6.25`; folder created 2025-10-03T17:02:57Z and the agenda six seconds later — about 76 hours' notice. **No minutes exist for 10/6/25 and none were ever approved**: the approved 12/3/25 minutes list 9/17, 10/1, 10/15, 11/5 and 11/19, and omit 10/6.

## Method and caveats

-   Inventory taken from the Drive web interface on 2026-08-18, signed in as [kevin.tyson@gmail.com](mailto:kevin.tyson@gmail.com). Contents will change as the district posts new packets; re-check before publishing.
-   Remote video links taken from the Cablecast gallery on 2026-08-19 (all five meetings sat on page 1 of its 257 items). Each match was confirmed against the show's title and event date in the site's own metadata (`/CablecastAPI/v1/shows/<id>`); gallery titles are reproduced exactly, punctuation quirks included. The 8/19/26 board meeting was matched the same way on 2026-08-22 (show 17529, event date 2026-08-19).
-   Matching is by dates in folder and file names. Document contents were not opened, so a misdated upload would not be caught.
-   File names are reproduced exactly as they appear in Drive, typos included (`FY26 Expeditures`, `3 .Claremont`).
-   The two finance meetings have nothing in this share. If the district posts finance-committee packets elsewhere, that location is not linked from this folder.
-   **Some sections have no local video.** Only the diarized transcript and the attributed dialogue CSV are on hand, so the `Video:` line names those instead of a file in `Input/Videos/`. The recording itself is reachable through the `Remote video:` link.
-   **Cablecast `eventDate` is not always the meeting date.** Five shows disagree with every other source — 16253 (`eventDate` 1/6/25 vs. a 1/7/25 meeting), 16315 (1/30/25 vs. 2/1/25), 16872 (9/26/25 vs. 10/1/25), and **17241 (3/17/26 vs. a 3/18/26 meeting) and 17291 (3/31/26 vs. a 4/1/26 meeting), both found 2026-08-28** — each off by exactly one day. In each case the show title, the transcript file name and the district's own dated packet folder agree with one another, and that date was used. Gallery titles carry the opposite failure — the wrong *year* (`1/6/24`, `11/19/26`, `1/7/25`, `1/20/25`, and from the 2023–24 backfill `School Board Meeting 1/3/23` for a 1/3/**24** meeting, `School Board Finance Meeting 1/5/23` for 1/5/**24**, and `SAU 6 Board Meeting 1/11/23` for 1/11/**24**) — so titles are reproduced verbatim as identifiers but never relied on for dating.
-   Show IDs for the backlog were read from the transcript file names (`16246 SchoolBoardFinance010625` → show 16246) and every one was confirmed against `/CablecastAPI/v1/shows/<id>` on 2026-08-23. None was guessed.
-   Minutes frequently sit in a *later* meeting's packet folder as a draft before reaching the Meeting Minutes share; where that is the only copy (10/1/25, and the SAU 6 meetings) the section says so.
-   **Every SAU 6 minutes file in Drive is a DRAFT, and the trail stops at the 9/4/25 draft.** No approved SAU 6 minutes exist in either share, and the Claremont Meeting Minutes share carries no SAU 6 file at all — its 2024 (26 files), 2025 (30), 2026 (20) and 2027 (2) year folders were all re-read on 2026-08-26 and every entry is a CSB document. The meetings of 12/3/24, 12/12/24, 9/11/25, 10/6/25, 11/13/25, 4/9/26 and 5/28/26 have no minutes of any kind on hand. *(added 8/26/26)*
-   **8/21/25 is filed twice under different bodies.** The SAU 6 share holds `9. DRAFT SAU6 Board Meeting Minutes 8.21 (1).25_` while the Claremont Minutes share holds `04. Approved CSB Meeting Minutes 8.21.25`. Whether that is one gathering recorded twice or two separate meetings was not determined; document contents were not opened. The same ambiguity affects 10/6/25. *(added 8/26/26)*
-   The 4/9/26 and 5/28/26 agendas are **live Google Docs, not PDFs**, so their content can change after the meeting; the links above resolve to whatever the document says today. *(added 8/26/26)*
-   No recording could be found in the Cablecast gallery for the 12/3/24, 8/21/25, 8/26/25 or 5/28/26 SAU 6 meetings. The site's search API was queried on 2026-08-26 for each date and for the terms `SAU`, `SAU6`, `Board Meeting`, `School Board`, `Meeting`, `Special`, `Superintendent` and `Budget Hearing` (≈200 shows scanned); the only shows carrying those event dates belong to other bodies. *(added 8/26/26)*
-   **This run covered Drive only.** The 74 sections added on 2026-08-28 carry no `Remote video:` line, because Cablecast matching belongs to the `full-map-update` skill rather than this one. Run that skill to fill them in.
-   **`Claremont School Board 81926 copy.mp4` was a byte-identical duplicate** of `Input/Videos/Claremont School Board 81926.mp4` (both 7,028,140,594 bytes), deliberately not mapped as a separate meeting; moved to `Output/_to_delete/` on 2026-09-02. *(added 8/28/26)*
-   **Finance Committee meetings have nothing in any district share, and that was checked three ways for each of the nine in 2023–24** (11/30/23, 12/1/23, 12/6/23, 12/13/23, 12/18/23, 1/5/24, 11/19/24, 12/13/24, 12/18/24): the packets archive for that year, the next board meeting's packet folder, and both candidate year folders in the Meeting Minutes share, plus Drive keyword searches on `finance`, `"Finance Subcommittee"` and each quoted dotted date. The only exception anywhere in the corpus is `Exhibit B- Meeting Minutes Finance Subcommittee 7.22.24`, filed as a board exhibit inside the 8/21/24 packet. *(added 8/28/26)*
-   **SAU 6 minutes follow their own filing rule**, confirmed across 2023–24: the draft is filed inside the *next* meeting's packet folder, and often a second copy sits in the meeting's own folder. Nothing SAU 6 ever reaches the Claremont Meeting Minutes share. 12/7/23 and 4/11/24 have no minutes anywhere. *(added 8/28/26)*
-   **Two 2024 minutes filenames say `CSB` for meetings this map treats as SAU 6 or joint** — `8. Approved CSB meeting minutes 11.14.24` (the 11/14/24 SAU 6 board meeting) and `5. Approved minutes for Joint CSB_City Council meeting 9.30.24`. Contents were not opened, so the body each actually records is unverified. *(added 8/28/26)*
-   **Four SAU 6 meetings still have no Cablecast recording** — 12/3/24, 8/21/25, 8/26/25 and 5/28/26. The gallery was searched for each on 2026-08-26 and nothing has appeared since; their sections carry no `Remote video:` line, which is the map's signal to re-check. *(re-confirmed 8/28/26)*
-   **Show ids for the 2023–24 and Feb–Jun 2026 sections were read from the video file names** (`15947 SchoolBoard082124.mp4` → show 15947) and **all 74 were confirmed against `/CablecastAPI/v1/shows/<id>?site=1` on 2026-08-28**. None was guessed. Gallery titles are reproduced exactly as the API returns them, including `SAU #6` vs `SAU 6`, `09/4/24`, and `School Board Meeting live 4/5/23`. *(added 8/28/26)*
-   **188 files in `Input/Videos/` were still unmapped after the 9/24/26 run.** 182 are meeting videos from 2015 through 2022 (2015: 2, 2016: 19, 2017: 31, 2018: 38, 2019: 29, 2020: 16, 2021: 23, 2022: 24, the last including `Claremont School Board 111722.mp4` and `SAU 6 Board Meeting live on 12122.mp4`). Only 2022 has a packet archive in Drive; nothing older exists in either share, and the Minutes share starts at 2023. Six are not meeting videos at all (two `.dmg` installers, a `.pdf`, a `.docx`, and an `audience_export_…` folder plus its `.zip`) and were not mapped. *(added 9/24/26)*
-   **The 2015 – 2022 backfill (9/26/26) closes that gap for all 180 numbered videos.** Sections with no packet folder say so in their body rather than carrying a per-meeting search log: the negative is structural (the packets archive's oldest year folder is `2022 Meeting Documents`, whose first folder is 7/20/22; the SAU 6 share's is `2022 Meeting Packets`, first folder 7/24/22; the Meeting Minutes share's oldest is `2023 Meeting Minutes`), re-verified live on 9/26/26. Pre-2023 minutes exist in Drive only as documents inside later packet folders, and each section that has one links it on a `Minutes on hand only as packet documents` line. Cablecast titles for these years are all-caps and often omit the year (`SCHOOL BOARD MEETING 5-16`); they are reproduced verbatim, double spaces included. *(added 9/26/26)*
